LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [p0].[IdProduct], [p0].[IdKeyFeature], [p0].[Id], [p0].[Name], [k].[Id] AS [Id0], [k].[CreateKeyFeature], [k].[Name] AS [Name0]

SELECT [p].[Id], [p].[BriefDiscription], [p].[Color], [p].[CreateRoverMechTime], [p].[FullDiscription], [p].[Images], [p].[Lastprise], [p].[MainCategoryId], [p].[Name], [p].[Prise], [p].[Quantity], [p].[Rating], [p].[SkuNumber], [p].[Status], [p].[Time_of_chenge], [m].[Id], [s].[IdProduct], [s].[IdKeyFeature], [s].[Id], [s].[Name], [s].[Id0], [s].[CreateKeyFeature], [s].[Name0], [m].[Name], [s0].[Id], [s0].[CategoryId], [s0].[ProductId], [s0].[Property], [s0].[Value], [s0].[Id0], [s0].[Name], [p2].[Id], [p2].[MainPhoto], [p2].[NamePicture], [p2].[ProductId], [p2].[Putch], [p2].[TimeEditFile]
FROM [Products] AS [p]
INNER JOIN [MainCategoryes] AS [m] ON [p].[MainCategoryId] = [m].[Id]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [p0].[IdProduct], [p0].[IdKeyFeature], [p0].[Id], [p0].[Name], [k].[Id] AS [Id0], [k].[CreateKeyFeature], [k].[Name] AS [Name0]
    FROM [ProductKeyFeatures] AS [p0]
    INNER JOIN [KeyFeatures] AS [k] ON [p0].[IdKeyFeature] = [k].[Id]
) AS [s] ON [p].[Id] = [s].[IdProduct]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [p1].[Id], [p1].[CategoryId], [p1].[ProductId], [p1].[Property], [p1].[Value], [c].[Id] AS [Id0], [c].[Name]
    FROM [PropertyValues] AS [p1]
    INNER JOIN [Categorys] AS [c] ON [p1].[CategoryId] = [c].[Id]
) AS [s0] ON [p].[Id] = [s0].[ProductId]
LEFT JOIN [Photoproducts] AS [p2] ON [p].[Id] = [p2].[ProductId]
ORDER BY [p].[Id], [m].[Id], [s].[IdProduct], [s].[IdKeyFeature], [s].[Id0], [s0].[Id], [s0].[Id0]
